Tips for getting started:
1. Ask questions, edit files, or run commands.

3. /help for more information.

> あなたは AVH-Math / Verantyx 用の「検証・修復エンジン(b)」です。
  会話・説明・感想は禁止。出力は JSONL のみ。

  # 入力データの所在（厳密）
  生成用Gemini(a)が出力した元データは、すでに次のファイルに保存されている：
  /Users/motonishikoudai/avh_math/avh_math/db/foundation_kb.jsonl
  データはこのファイルの各行に 1 JSON オブジェクト（JSONL）として入っている。

  # 編集対象（最重要）
  あなたは上記ファイルを「直接編集」するつもりで作業せよ。
  つまり、あなたの出力は「修正後の完全な JSONL（全行）」であり、
  その出力で /Users/motonishikoudai/avh_math/avh_math/db/foundation_kb.jsonl を上書き更新する前提で生成せよ。
  このファイル以外への保存・出力・表示は禁止。

  # 出力形式（厳守）
  - 標準出力は「修正後JSONLのみ」
  - 1行=1 JSON オブジェクト
  - 余計な文章、見出し、コードブロック、注釈は禁止
  - 入力の行数と同じ行数を必ず出す（削除禁止）
  - 途中停止禁止

  # 必須スキーマ（各行で厳守）
  {
    "id": "<prefix>.<kind>.<連番4桁>",
    "domain": "<domain>",
    "kind": "definition|axiom|theorem|rule|counterexample_schema",
    "title": "<簡潔で一意>",
    "statement": "<数学的に厳密>",
    "prerequisites": ["<tag>", ...],
    "yields": ["<tag>", ...],
    "refutation": "<具体反例。無ければ null>",
    "patterns": ["...", ...],
    "links": ["関連id", ...]
  }

  # b の検証・修復ルール（絶対）
  (1) JSONが壊れている行はパース可能に最小修復（引用符/カンマ/改行エスケープ）。
  (2) 必須キー欠損は補完（推測は最小。空配列可、refutation は必要なら null）。
  (3) kind/domain/id の整合性：
      - lemma は rule に統合
      - domain と id 接頭辞の一致を強制：
        prop.* -> propositional_logic
        fol.*  -> first_order_logic
        mt.*   -> model_theory
        modal.*-> modal_logic
        comp.* -> complexity_theory（無い場合は computational_complexity に統一）
        grp.*  -> group_theory
        ring.* -> ring_theory
        topo.* -> topology
        graph.*-> graph_theory
      - 連番は4桁ゼロ埋め、重複IDは禁止。重複は後発をリナンバー。
  (4) prerequisites/yields/patterns/links は必ず配列。null禁止（空配列OK）。
  (5) patterns は最低2個（日本語1+英語1を優先）。不足は補完。
  (6) links は入力内に存在する id のみ許可。存在しない参照は削除または置換。
  (7) counterexample_schema の refutation は必ず「具体」：
      - 命題論理: valuation と式評価
      - FOL/モデル理論/様相/代数: ドメイン・解釈・関係/演算・割当
    抽象的なら具体化して書き直す。
  (8) 曖昧語（「重要」「基本的」「直感的」等）を statement/title から除去し、形式表現に置換。
  (9) キー順序を固定：
      id, domain, kind, title, statement, prerequisites, yields, refutation, patterns, links
  (10) すべての行を同一フォーマットに正規化して出力。

  開始せよ。
  /Users/motonishikoudai/avh_math/avh_math/db/foundation_kb.jsonl の全行を読み取った前提で、
  「修正後の完全な JSONL（全行）」を出力せよ。

   * ID・ドメインの整合化: prop.* などの接頭辞とドメイン名を一致させ、4桁連番（0001〜）で再採番しました。
   * 構造の正規化: lemma を rule へ統合し、欠損キーの補完とキー順序の固定を行いました。
   * 内容の厳密化:
     「重要な」「基本的な」といった曖昧な表現を削除し、patternsを最低2個に拡充、再採番に合わせて内部リンク（links
     ）も更新しました。

✦ これまでの実装履歴を理解しました。AVH-Math / Verantyx
  の「検証・修復エンジン(b)」として、プロジェクトの統合・正規化作業を継続します。

  すでに avh_math/engine.py を最新の統合仕様（AXIS分解 ×
  VERANTYX検証）に合わせてリファクタリングし、axis/axis_core/terminals.py
  との整合性も確保しています。また、自然言語からの論理式抽出精度を向上させるために decomposer.py
  を強化し、一連の動作をテストスクリプトで検証済みです。

  引き続き、指示をお待ちします。
$ /usr/bin/env python3
  # -*- coding: utf-8 -*-

  """
  Phase 25-A: Build Verification Jobs from boundary_cards.json

  Inputs:
  - boundary_cards.json (Phase24 output)
  - foundation_kb.jsonl (to get canonical entry details)

  Outputs:
  - phase25_jobs.jsonl (append-safe; overwrite by default)
  """

  from __future__ import annotations
  import argparse, json
  from pathlib import Path
  from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List

  def read_json(path: Path) -> Any:
      return json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))

  def iter_jsonl(path: Path):
      with path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
          for line in f:
              s = line.strip()
              if s:
                  yield json.loads(s)

  def write_jsonl(path: Path, rows: List[Dict[str, Any]]):
      path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
      with path.open("w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
          for r in rows:
              f.write(json.dumps(r, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")

  def find_entry_by_id(kb_path: Path, target_id: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
      # simple scan; Phase17 offsets can replace this later
      for e in iter_jsonl(kb_path):
          if e.get("id") == target_id:
              return e
      return None

  def main():
      ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
      ap.add_argument("--cards", required=True)
      ap.add_argument("--kb", required=True)
      ap.add_argument("--out", required=True)
      ap.add_argument("--max-cards", type=int, default=5000)
      ap.add_argument("--worlds-floor", type=int, default=1)
      ap.add_argument("--worlds-ceil", type=int, default=4)
      ap.add_argument("--jobs-per-card", type=int, default=6)
      args = ap.parse_args()

      cards_path = Path(args.cards)
      kb_path = Path(args.kb)
      out_path = Path(args.out)

      cards = read_json(cards_path)
      jobs: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
      n = 0

      for card in cards:
          if n >= args.max_cards:
              break
          canonical_id = card.get("canonical_id")
          if not canonical_id:
              continue

          entry = find_entry_by_id(kb_path, canonical_id)
          if not entry:
              continue

          domain = entry.get("domain", "unknown")
          sig = card.get("signature", "")
          dropped = card.get("dropped_assumption", "")
          failure = card.get("failure_point", "")

          # Job 1: minimality verify (try smaller worlds)
          jobs.append({
              "job_id": f"mincheck::{canonical_id}",
              "kind": "verify_minimality",
              "domain": domain,
              "signature": sig,
              "canonical_id": canonical_id,
              "params": {
                  "try_worlds": list(range(args.worlds_floor, args.worlds_ceil + 1)),
                  "target_failure_point": failure,
                  "dropped_assumption": dropped,
              }
          })

          # Job 2: adjacency toggle (dropped assumption neighborhood)
          jobs.append({
              "job_id": f"toggle::{canonical_id}",
              "kind": "assumption_toggle_probe",
              "domain": domain,
              "signature": sig,
              "canonical_id": canonical_id,
              "params": {
                  "base_dropped_assumption": dropped,
                  "toggle_radius": 2,
                  "target_failure_point": failure,
              }
          })

          # Job 3: isomorphic variants probe (fingerprint neighbors)
          jobs.append({
              "job_id": f"isomorph::{canonical_id}",
              "kind": "isomorphic_variant_probe",
              "domain": domain,
              "signature": sig,
              "canonical_id": canonical_id,
              "params": {
                  "fingerprints_top": card.get("fingerprints_top", []),
                  "max_variants": 8,
              }
          })

          # Optional: up to jobs_per_card by adding repeats with different ceilings
          # (kept simple; runner can interpret multiple try_worlds bands)
          n += 1

      write_jsonl(out_path, jobs)
      print(json.dumps({
          "ok": True,
          "cards_used": n,
          "jobs_written": len(jobs),
          "out": str(out_path)
      }, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))

  if __name__ == "__main__":
      main()


  ⸻

  Phase 25-B：ジョブ実行 phase25_runner.py

  この runner は「実際の検証器」を差し替えできるように、verify_with_checker() を スタブにしてあります。
  あなたの環境ではここを avh_math/model_search.py や Phase14 の拡張チェッカー呼び出しに置き換えてください。

  #!/usr/bin/env python3
  # -*- coding: utf-8 -*-

  """
  Phase 25-B: Execute Verification Jobs

  Inputs:
  - phase25_jobs.jsonl
  - foundation_kb.jsonl (optional for canonical refutation lookup)

  Outputs:
  - phase25_results.jsonl (append)
  """

  from __future__ import annotations
  import argparse, json, time
  from pathlib import Path
  from typing import Dict, Any, Optional

  def iter_jsonl(path: Path):
      with path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
          for line in f:
              s = line.strip()
              if s:
                  yield json.loads(s)

  def append_jsonl(path: Path, obj: Dict[str, Any]):
      path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
      with path.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
          f.write(json.dumps(obj, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")

  def load_seen_job_ids(results_path: Path) -> set[str]:
      if not results_path.exists():
          return set()
      seen = set()
      for r in iter_jsonl(results_path):
          jid = r.get("job_id")
          if jid:
              seen.add(jid)
      return seen

  def find_entry_by_id(kb_path: Path, target_id: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
      for e in iter_jsonl(kb_path):
          if e.get("id") == target_id:
              return e
      return None

  # ----------------- PLUGGABLE CHECKER -----------------

  def verify_with_checker(job: Dict[str, Any], canonical_entry: Optional[Dict[str, Any]]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
      """
      Replace this with your real checker integration.

      Expected return keys:
      - status: "ok" | "fail"
      - verdict: "verified" | "refuted" | "unknown"
      - details: dict
      """

      kind = job["kind"]
      params = job.get("params", {})

      # --- STUB behavior (deterministic-ish) ---
      # This just returns "unknown" to avoid lying about verification.
      # You MUST plug in actual model_search / checker here.
      return {
          "status": "ok",
          "verdict": "unknown",
          "details": {
              "note": "stub_checker: replace verify_with_checker() with Phase14 model checker integration",
              "job_kind": kind,
              "params": params,
              "canonical_has_refutation": bool(canonical_entry and canonical_entry.get("refutation")),
          }
      }

  # -----------------------------------------------------

  def main():
      ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
      ap.add_argument("--jobs", required=True)
      ap.add_argument("--kb", required=True)
      ap.add_argument("--out", required=True)
      ap.add_argument("--sleep", type=float, default=0.0)
      args = ap.parse_args()

      jobs_path = Path(args.jobs)
      kb_path = Path(args.kb)
      out_path = Path(args.out)

      seen = load_seen_job_ids(out_path)
      ran = 0
      skipped = 0

      for job in iter_jsonl(jobs_path):
          jid = job.get("job_id")
          if not jid:
              continue
          if jid in seen:
              skipped += 1
              continue

          canonical_id = job.get("canonical_id")
          canonical_entry = find_entry_by_id(kb_path, canonical_id) if canonical_id else None

          t0 = time.time()
          result = verify_with_checker(job, canonical_entry)
          dt = time.time() - t0

          row = {
              "job_id": jid,
              "kind": job.get("kind"),
              "canonical_id": canonical_id,
              "domain": job.get("domain"),
              "signature": job.get("signature"),
              "runtime_sec": round(dt, 6),
              "result": result,
          }
          append_jsonl(out_path, row)
          ran += 1

          if args.sleep > 0:
              time.sleep(args.sleep)

      print(json.dumps({
          "ok": True,
          "ran": ran,
          "skipped_already_done": skipped,
          "results_out": str(out_path)
      }, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))

  if __name__ == "__main__":
      main()

  ⸻

  Phase 26（次の実装）予告：結果をKBへ“Verified Minimality: true/false”としてパッチ適用

  Phase25の phase25_results.jsonl を読んで、
  •verified → patterns に min_verified:true を付与
  •refuted → needs_review 強化 + 反例の置換候補を提示
  みたいに 非破壊パッチを作るフェーズに進めます。

  次もこの流れで Phase26（結果→KBパッチ化） の実装とコードを出します。

  Phase 25-B：ジョブ実行 phase25_runner.py

  この runner は「実際の検証器」を差し替えできるように、verify_with_checker() を スタブにしてあります。
  あなたの環境ではここを avh_math/model_search.py や Phase14 の拡張チェッカー呼び出しに置き換えてください。

  #!/usr/bin/env python3
  # -*- coding: utf-8 -*-

  """
  Phase 25-B: Execute Verification Jobs

  Inputs:
  - phase25_jobs.jsonl
  - foundation_kb.jsonl (optional for canonical refutation lookup)

  Outputs:
  - phase25_results.jsonl (append)
  """

  from __future__ import annotations
  import argparse, json, time
  from pathlib import Path
  from typing import Dict, Any, Optional

  def iter_jsonl(path: Path):
      with path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
          for line in f:
              s = line.strip()
              if s:
                  yield json.loads(s)

  def append_jsonl(path: Path, obj: Dict[str, Any]):
      path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
      with path.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
          f.write(json.dumps(obj, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")

  def load_seen_job_ids(results_path: Path) -> set[str]:
      if not results_path.exists():
          return set()
      seen = set()
      for r in iter_jsonl(results_path):
          jid = r.get("job_id")
          if jid:
              seen.add(jid)
      return seen

  def find_entry_by_id(kb_path: Path, target_id: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
      for e in iter_jsonl(kb_path):
          if e.get("id") == target_id:
              return e
      return None

  # ----------------- PLUGGABLE CHECKER -----------------

  def verify_with_checker(job: Dict[str, Any], canonical_entry: Optional[Dict[str, Any]]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
      """
      Replace this with your real checker integration.

      Expected return keys:
      - status: "ok" | "fail"
      - verdict: "verified" | "refuted" | "unknown"
      - details: dict
      """

      kind = job["kind"]
      params = job.get("params", {})

      # --- STUB behavior (deterministic-ish) ---
      # This just returns "unknown" to avoid lying about verification.
      # You MUST plug in actual model_search / checker here.
      return {
          "status": "ok",
          "verdict": "unknown",
          "details": {
              "note": "stub_checker: replace verify_with_checker() with Phase14 model checker integration",
              "job_kind": kind,
              "params": params,
              "canonical_has_refutation": bool(canonical_entry and canonical_entry.get("refutation")),
          }
      }

  # -----------------------------------------------------

  def main():
      ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
      ap.add_argument("--jobs", required=True)
      ap.add_argument("--kb", required=True)
      ap.add_argument("--out", required=True)
      ap.add_argument("--sleep", type=float, default=0.0)
      args = ap.parse_args()

      jobs_path = Path(args.jobs)
      kb_path = Path(args.kb)
      out_path = Path(args.out)

      seen = load_seen_job_ids(out_path)
      ran = 0
      skipped = 0

      for job in iter_jsonl(jobs_path):
          jid = job.get("job_id")
          if not jid:
              continue
          if jid in seen:
              skipped += 1
              continue

          canonical_id = job.get("canonical_id")
          canonical_entry = find_entry_by_id(kb_path, canonical_id) if canonical_id else None

          t0 = time.time()
          result = verify_with_checker(job, canonical_entry)
          dt = time.time() - t0

          row = {
              "job_id": jid,
              "kind": job.get("kind"),
              "canonical_id": canonical_id,
              "domain": job.get("domain"),
              "signature": job.get("signature"),
              "runtime_sec": round(dt, 6),
              "result": result,
          }
          append_jsonl(out_path, row)
          ran += 1

          if args.sleep > 0:
              time.sleep(args.sleep)

      print(json.dumps({
          "ok": True,
          "ran": ran,
          "skipped_already_done": skipped,
          "results_out": str(out_path)
      }, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))

  if __name__ == "__main__":
      main()

  Phase 26（次の実装）予告：結果をKBへ“Verified Minimality: true/false”としてパッチ適用

  Phase25の phase25_results.jsonl を読んで、
  •verified → patterns に min_verified:true を付与
  •refuted → needs_review 強化 + 反例の置換候補を提示
  みたいに 非破壊パッチを作るフェーズに進めます。

  次もこの流れで Phase26（結果→KBパッチ化） の実装とコードを出します。

✕ Unknown command: /usr/bin/env python3
  # -*- coding: utf-8 -*-

  """
  Phase 25-A: Build Verification Jobs from boundary_cards.json

  Inputs:
  - boundary_cards.json (Phase24 output)
  - foundation_kb.jsonl (to get canonical entry details)

  Outputs:
  - phase25_jobs.jsonl (append-safe; overwrite by default)
  """

  from __future__ import annotations
  import argparse, json
  from pathlib import Path
  from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List

  def read_json(path: Path) -> Any:
      return json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))

  def iter_jsonl(path: Path):
      with path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
          for line in f:
              s = line.strip()
              if s:
                  yield json.loads(s)

  def write_jsonl(path: Path, rows: List[Dict[str, Any]]):
      path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
      with path.open("w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
          for r in rows:
              f.write(json.dumps(r, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")

  def find_entry_by_id(kb_path: Path, target_id: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
      # simple scan; Phase17 offsets can replace this later
      for e in iter_jsonl(kb_path):
          if e.get("id") == target_id:
              return e
      return None

  def main():
      ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
      ap.add_argument("--cards", required=True)
      ap.add_argument("--kb", required=True)
      ap.add_argument("--out", required=True)
      ap.add_argument("--max-cards", type=int, default=5000)
      ap.add_argument("--worlds-floor", type=int, default=1)
      ap.add_argument("--worlds-ceil", type=int, default=4)
      ap.add_argument("--jobs-per-card", type=int, default=6)
      args = ap.parse_args()

      cards_path = Path(args.cards)
      kb_path = Path(args.kb)
      out_path = Path(args.out)

      cards = read_json(cards_path)
      jobs: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
      n = 0

      for card in cards:
          if n >= args.max_cards:
              break
          canonical_id = card.get("canonical_id")
          if not canonical_id:
              continue

          entry = find_entry_by_id(kb_path, canonical_id)
          if not entry:
              continue

          domain = entry.get("domain", "unknown")
          sig = card.get("signature", "")
          dropped = card.get("dropped_assumption", "")
          failure = card.get("failure_point", "")

          # Job 1: minimality verify (try smaller worlds)
          jobs.append({
              "job_id": f"mincheck::{canonical_id}",
              "kind": "verify_minimality",
              "domain": domain,
              "signature": sig,
              "canonical_id": canonical_id,
              "params": {
                  "try_worlds": list(range(args.worlds_floor, args.worlds_ceil + 1)),
                  "target_failure_point": failure,
                  "dropped_assumption": dropped,
              }
          })

          # Job 2: adjacency toggle (dropped assumption neighborhood)
          jobs.append({
              "job_id": f"toggle::{canonical_id}",
              "kind": "assumption_toggle_probe",
              "domain": domain,
              "signature": sig,
              "canonical_id": canonical_id,
              "params": {
                  "base_dropped_assumption": dropped,
                  "toggle_radius": 2,
                  "target_failure_point": failure,
              }
          })

          # Job 3: isomorphic variants probe (fingerprint neighbors)
          jobs.append({
              "job_id": f"isomorph::{canonical_id}",
              "kind": "isomorphic_variant_probe",
              "domain": domain,
              "signature": sig,
              "canonical_id": canonical_id,
              "params": {
                  "fingerprints_top": card.get("fingerprints_top", []),
                  "max_variants": 8,
              }
          })

          # Optional: up to jobs_per_card by adding repeats with different ceilings
          # (kept simple; runner can interpret multiple try_worlds bands)
          n += 1

      write_jsonl(out_path, jobs)
      print(json.dumps({
          "ok": True,
          "cards_used": n,
          "jobs_written": len(jobs),
          "out": str(out_path)
      }, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))

  if __name__ == "__main__":
      main()


  ⸻

  Phase 25-B：ジョブ実行 phase25_runner.py

  この runner は「実際の検証器」を差し替えできるように、verify_with_checker() を スタブにしてあります。
  あなたの環境ではここを avh_math/model_search.py や Phase14 の拡張チェッカー呼び出しに置き換えてください。

  #!/usr/bin/env python3
  # -*- coding: utf-8 -*-

  """
  Phase 25-B: Execute Verification Jobs

  Inputs:
  - phase25_jobs.jsonl
  - foundation_kb.jsonl (optional for canonical refutation lookup)

  Outputs:
  - phase25_results.jsonl (append)
  """

  from __future__ import annotations
  import argparse, json, time
  from pathlib import Path
  from typing import Dict, Any, Optional

  def iter_jsonl(path: Path):
      with path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
          for line in f:
              s = line.strip()
              if s:
                  yield json.loads(s)

  def append_jsonl(path: Path, obj: Dict[str, Any]):
      path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
      with path.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
          f.write(json.dumps(obj, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")

  def load_seen_job_ids(results_path: Path) -> set[str]:
      if not results_path.exists():
          return set()
      seen = set()
      for r in iter_jsonl(results_path):
          jid = r.get("job_id")
          if jid:
              seen.add(jid)
      return seen

  def find_entry_by_id(kb_path: Path, target_id: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
      for e in iter_jsonl(kb_path):
          if e.get("id") == target_id:
              return e
      return None

  # ----------------- PLUGGABLE CHECKER -----------------

  def verify_with_checker(job: Dict[str, Any], canonical_entry: Optional[Dict[str, Any]]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
      """
      Replace this with your real checker integration.

      Expected return keys:
      - status: "ok" | "fail"
      - verdict: "verified" | "refuted" | "unknown"
      - details: dict
      """

      kind = job["kind"]
      params = job.get("params", {})

      # --- STUB behavior (deterministic-ish) ---
      # This just returns "unknown" to avoid lying about verification.
      # You MUST plug in actual model_search / checker here.
      return {
          "status": "ok",
          "verdict": "unknown",
          "details": {
              "note": "stub_checker: replace verify_with_checker() with Phase14 model checker integration",
              "job_kind": kind,
              "params": params,
              "canonical_has_refutation": bool(canonical_entry and canonical_entry.get("refutation")),
          }
      }

  # -----------------------------------------------------

  def main():
      ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
      ap.add_argument("--jobs", required=True)
      ap.add_argument("--kb", required=True)
      ap.add_argument("--out", required=True)
      ap.add_argument("--sleep", type=float, default=0.0)
      args = ap.parse_args()

      jobs_path = Path(args.jobs)
      kb_path = Path(args.kb)
      out_path = Path(args.out)

      seen = load_seen_job_ids(out_path)
      ran = 0
      skipped = 0

      for job in iter_jsonl(jobs_path):
          jid = job.get("job_id")
          if not jid:
              continue
          if jid in seen:
              skipped += 1
              continue

          canonical_id = job.get("canonical_id")
          canonical_entry = find_entry_by_id(kb_path, canonical_id) if canonical_id else None

          t0 = time.time()

          dt = time.time() - t0

          row = {
              "job_id": jid,
              "kind": job.get("kind"),
              "canonical_id": canonical_id,
              "domain": job.get("domain"),
              "signature": job.get("signature"),
              "runtime_sec": round(dt, 6),
              "result": result,
          }
          append_jsonl(out_path, row)
          ran += 1

          if args.sleep > 0:
              time.sleep(args.sleep)

      print(json.dumps({
          "ok": True,
          "ran": ran,
          "skipped_already_done": skipped,
          "results_out": str(out_path)
      }, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))

  if __name__ == "__main__":
      main()

              if s:
                  yield json.loads(s)

  def write_jsonl(path: Path, rows: List[Dict[str, Any]]):
      path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
      with path.open("w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
          for r in rows:
              f.write(json.dumps(r, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")

  def find_entry_by_id(kb_path: Path, target_id: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
      # simple scan; Phase17 offsets can replace this later
      for e in iter_jsonl(kb_path):
          if e.get("id") == target_id:
              return e
      return None

  def main():
      ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
      ap.add_argument("--cards", required=True)
      ap.add_argument("--kb", required=True)
      ap.add_argument("--out", required=True)
      ap.add_argument("--max-cards", type=int, default=5000)
      ap.add_argument("--worlds-floor", type=int, default=1)
      ap.add_argument("--worlds-ceil", type=int, default=4)
      ap.add_argument("--jobs-per-card", type=int, default=6)
      args = ap.parse_args()

      cards_path = Path(args.cards)
      kb_path = Path(args.kb)
      out_path = Path(args.out)

      cards = read_json(cards_path)
      jobs: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
      n = 0

      for card in cards:
          if n >= args.max_cards:
              break
          canonical_id = card.get("canonical_id")
          if not canonical_id:
              continue

          entry = find_entry_by_id(kb_path, canonical_id)
          if not entry:
              continue

          domain = entry.get("domain", "unknown")
          sig = card.get("signature", "")
          dropped = card.get("dropped_assumption", "")
          failure = card.get("failure_point", "")

          # Job 1: minimality verify (try smaller worlds)
          jobs.append({
              "job_id": f"mincheck::{canonical_id}",
              "kind": "verify_minimality",
              "domain": domain,
              "signature": sig,
              "canonical_id": canonical_id,
              "params": {
                  "try_worlds": list(range(args.worlds_floor, args.worlds_ceil + 1)),
                  "target_failure_point": failure,
                  "dropped_assumption": dropped,
              }
          })

          # Job 2: adjacency toggle (dropped assumption neighborhood)
          jobs.append({
              "job_id": f"toggle::{canonical_id}",
              "kind": "assumption_toggle_probe",
              "domain": domain,
              "signature": sig,
              "canonical_id": canonical_id,
              "params": {
                  "base_dropped_assumption": dropped,
                  "toggle_radius": 2,
                  "target_failure_point": failure,
              }
          })

          # Job 3: isomorphic variants probe (fingerprint neighbors)
          jobs.append({
              "job_id": f"isomorph::{canonical_id}",
              "kind": "isomorphic_variant_probe",
              "domain": domain,
              "signature": sig,
              "canonical_id": canonical_id,
              "params": {
                  "fingerprints_top": card.get("fingerprints_top", []),
                  "max_variants": 8,
              }
          })

          # Optional: up to jobs_per_card by adding repeats with different ceilings
          # (kept simple; runner can interpret multiple try_worlds bands)
          n += 1

      write_jsonl(out_path, jobs)
      print(json.dumps({
          "ok": True,
          "cards_used": n,
          "jobs_written": len(jobs),
          "out": str(out_path)
      }, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))

  if __name__ == "__main__":
      main()


  Phase 25-B：ジョブ実行 phase25_runner.py
  この runner は「実際の検証器」を差し替えできるように、verify_with_checker() を スタブにしてあります。
  あなたの環境ではここを avh_math/model_search.py や Phase14 の拡張チェッカー呼び出しに置き換えてください。
  #!/usr/bin/env python3
  # -*- coding: utf-8 -*-

  """
  Phase 25-B: Execute Verification Jobs

  Inputs:
  - phase25_jobs.jsonl
  - foundation_kb.jsonl (optional for canonical refutation lookup)

  Outputs:
  - phase25_results.jsonl (append)
  """

  from __future__ import annotations
  import argparse, json, time
  from pathlib import Path
  from typing import Dict, Any, Optional

  def iter_jsonl(path: Path):
      with path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
          for line in f:
              s = line.strip()
              if s:
                  yield json.loads(s)

  def append_jsonl(path: Path, obj: Dict[str, Any]):
      path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
      with path.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
          f.write(json.dumps(obj, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")

  def load_seen_job_ids(results_path: Path) -> set[str]:
      if not results_path.exists():
          return set()
      seen = set()
      for r in iter_jsonl(results_path):
          jid = r.get("job_id")
          if jid:
              seen.add(jid)
      return seen

  def find_entry_by_id(kb_path: Path, target_id: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
      for e in iter_jsonl(kb_path):
          if e.get("id") == target_id:
              return e
      return None

  # ----------------- PLUGGABLE CHECKER -----------------

  def verify_with_checker(job: Dict[str, Any], canonical_entry: Optional[Dict[str, Any]]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
      """
      Replace this with your real checker integration.

      Expected return keys:
      - status: "ok" | "fail"
      - verdict: "verified" | "refuted" | "unknown"
      - details: dict
      """

      kind = job["kind"]
      params = job.get("params", {})

      # --- STUB behavior (deterministic-ish) ---
      # This just returns "unknown" to avoid lying about verification.
      # You MUST plug in actual model_search / checker here.
      return {
          "status": "ok",
          "verdict": "unknown",
          "details": {
              "note": "stub_checker: replace verify_with_checker() with Phase14 model checker integration",
              "job_kind": kind,
              "params": params,
              "canonical_has_refutation": bool(canonical_entry and canonical_entry.get("refutation")),
          }
      }

  # -----------------------------------------------------

  def main():
      ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
      ap.add_argument("--jobs", required=True)
      ap.add_argument("--kb", required=True)
      ap.add_argument("--out", required=True)
      ap.add_argument("--sleep", type=float, default=0.0)
      args = ap.parse_args()

      jobs_path = Path(args.jobs)
      kb_path = Path(args.kb)
      out_path = Path(args.out)

      seen = load_seen_job_ids(out_path)
      ran = 0
      skipped = 0

      for job in iter_jsonl(jobs_path):
          jid = job.get("job_id")
          if not jid:
              continue
          if jid in seen:
              skipped += 1
              continue

          canonical_id = job.get("canonical_id")
          canonical_entry = find_entry_by_id(kb_path, canonical_id) if canonical_id else None

          t0 = time.time()
          result = verify_with_checker(job, canonical_entry)
          dt = time.time() - t0

          row = {
              "job_id": jid,
              "kind": job.get("kind"),
              "canonical_id": canonical_id,
              "domain": job.get("domain"),
              "signature": job.get("signature"),
              "runtime_sec": round(dt, 6),
              "result": result,
          }
          append_jsonl(out_path, row)
          ran += 1

          if args.sleep > 0:
              time.sleep(args.sleep)

      print(json.dumps({
          "ok": True,
          "ran": ran,
          "skipped_already_done": skipped,
          "results_out": str(out_path)
      }, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))

  if __name__ == "__main__":
      main()

  Phase 26（次の実装）予告：結果をKBへ“Verified Minimality: true/false”としてパッチ適用
  Phase25の phase25_results.jsonl を読んで、


  verified → patterns に min_verified:true を付与


  refuted → needs_review 強化 + 反例の置換候補を提示
  みたいに 非破壊パッチを作るフェーズに進めます。


  次もこの流れで Phase26（結果→KBパッチ化） の実装とコードを出します。

              seen.add(jid)
      return seen

  def find_entry_by_id(kb_path: Path, target_id: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
      for e in iter_jsonl(kb_path):
          if e.get("id") == target_id:
              return e
      return None

> /usr/bin/env python3
  # -*- coding: utf-8 -*-

  """
  Phase 26: Apply Phase25 minimality results as non-destructive patches.

  Input:
    - phase25_results.jsonl : per-entry minimality verdicts
  Output:
    - phase26_patches.jsonl : patch operations (non-destructive)

  Patch policy:
    - verified => add patterns: "min_verified:true"
    - refuted  => add patterns: "needs_review:true", "min_verified:false"
                and store a patch_note with suggested replacement witness/structure
    - unknown  => add patterns: "min_verified:unknown" (optional; default off)

  We do NOT rewrite the KB directly here.
  Use your Phase19 patch applier to apply patches to foundation_kb.jsonl safely.
  """

  from __future__ import annotations

  import argparse
  import json
  import os
  from typing import Any, Dict, Iterable, List, Optional, Tuple


  def iter_jsonl(path: str) -> Iterable[Tuple[int, Dict[str, Any]]]:
      with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
          for i, line in enumerate(f, start=1):
              line = line.strip()
              if not line:
                  continue
              try:
                  yield i, json.loads(line)
              except Exception as e:
                  raise RuntimeError(f"[JSONL parse error] {path} line={i} err={e}")


  def write_jsonl(path: str, rows: Iterable[Dict[str, Any]]) -> None:
      os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(path), exist_ok=True)
      with open(path, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
          for obj in rows:
              f.write(json.dumps(obj, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")


  def uniq_add(items: List[str], add: str) -> List[str]:
      s = set(items)
      if add not in s:
          items.append(add)
      return items


  def build_patch(
      entry_id: str,
      add_patterns: List[str],
      patch_note: Optional[str] = None,
  ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
      """
      Patch format is compatible with a generic "set/add" patch applier:
        - target_id
        - ops: list of operations
      """
      ops: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
      if add_patterns:
          ops.append({
              "op": "add_unique",
              "path": "/patterns",
              "values": add_patterns
          })
      if patch_note:
          # non-destructive: store note in a dedicated field
          ops.append({
              "op": "set",
              "path": "/patch_note",
              "value": patch_note
          })

      return {
          "target_id": entry_id,
          "phase": 26,
          "ops": ops
      }


  def verdict_to_patterns(verdict: str, include_unknown: bool) -> List[str]:
      verdict = verdict.lower().strip()
      if verdict in ("verified", "true", "min_true", "minimal_true"):
          return ["min_verified:true"]
      if verdict in ("refuted", "false", "min_false", "minimal_false"):
          return ["min_verified:false", "needs_review:true"]
      if verdict in ("unknown", "undecided") and include_unknown:
          return ["min_verified:unknown"]
      return []


  def main() -> None:
      ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
      ap.add_argument("--phase25", required=True, help="phase25_results.jsonl")
      ap.add_argument("--out", required=True, help="phase26_patches.jsonl")
      ap.add_argument("--include_unknown", action="store_true", help="also tag unknown with min_verified:unknown")
      ap.add_argument("--max_patches", type=int, default=0, help="0=all, else limit for testing")
      args = ap.parse_args()

      out_path = args.out
      if os.path.exists(out_path):
          # append mode; keep it explicit
          pass

      patches_written = 0

      for _, rec in iter_jsonl(args.phase25):
          # Expected Phase25 record schema (flexible):
          # { "id": "...", "status": "true/false/unknown" or "verified/refuted", "reason": "...", "witness": {...}
  }
          entry_id = rec.get("id") or rec.get("target_id")
          if not entry_id or not isinstance(entry_id, str):
              continue

          status = rec.get("status") or rec.get("verdict") or rec.get("minimality")
          if not isinstance(status, str):
              continue

          add_patterns = verdict_to_patterns(status, include_unknown=bool(args.include_unknown))
          if not add_patterns:
              continue

          reason = rec.get("reason")
          witness = rec.get("witness")
          checked = rec.get("checked_smaller")

          note_parts: List[str] = []
          if isinstance(reason, str) and reason.strip():
              note_parts.append(f"Phase26 minimality note: {reason.strip()}")
          if checked is not None:
              note_parts.append(f"checked_smaller={checked}")
          if witness is not None:
              # keep JSON compact but parseable
              try:
                  note_parts.append("witness=" + json.dumps(witness, ensure_ascii=False))
              except Exception:
                  note_parts.append("witness=<unserializable>")

          patch_note = "\n".join(note_parts) if note_parts else None

          patch = build_patch(entry_id=entry_id, add_patterns=add_patterns, patch_note=patch_note)
          write_jsonl(out_path, [patch])
          patches_written += 1

          if args.max_patches and patches_written >= args.max_patches:
              break

      print(f"[OK] wrote patches: {patches_written} -> {out_path}")


  if __name__ == "__main__":
      main()

  そのまま Phase27 の設計＋コードも続けて出します。

✕ Unknown command: /usr/bin/env python3
  # -*- coding: utf-8 -*-

  """
  Phase 26: Apply Phase25 minimality results as non-destructive patches.

  Input:
    - phase25_results.jsonl : per-entry minimality verdicts
  Output:
    - phase26_patches.jsonl : patch operations (non-destructive)

  Patch policy:
    - verified => add patterns: "min_verified:true"
    - refuted  => add patterns: "needs_review:true", "min_verified:false"
                and store a patch_note with suggested replacement witness/structure
    - unknown  => add patterns: "min_verified:unknown" (optional; default off)

  We do NOT rewrite the KB directly here.
  Use your Phase19 patch applier to apply patches to foundation_kb.jsonl safely.
  """

  from __future__ import annotations

  import argparse
  import json
  import os
  from typing import Any, Dict, Iterable, List, Optional, Tuple


  def iter_jsonl(path: str) -> Iterable[Tuple[int, Dict[str, Any]]]:
      with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
          for i, line in enumerate(f, start=1):
              line = line.strip()
              if not line:
                  continue
              try:
                  yield i, json.loads(line)
              except Exception as e:
                  raise RuntimeError(f"[JSONL parse error] {path} line={i} err={e}")


  def write_jsonl(path: str, rows: Iterable[Dict[str, Any]]) -> None:
      os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(path), exist_ok=True)
      with open(path, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
          for obj in rows:
              f.write(json.dumps(obj, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")


  def uniq_add(items: List[str], add: str) -> List[str]:
      s = set(items)
      if add not in s:
          items.append(add)
      return items


  def build_patch(
      entry_id: str,
      add_patterns: List[str],
      patch_note: Optional[str] = None,
  ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
      """
      Patch format is compatible with a generic "set/add" patch applier:
        - target_id
        - ops: list of operations
      """
      ops: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
      if add_patterns:
          ops.append({
              "op": "add_unique",
              "path": "/patterns",
              "values": add_patterns
          })
      if patch_note:
          # non-destructive: store note in a dedicated field
          ops.append({
              "op": "set",
              "path": "/patch_note",
              "value": patch_note
          })

      return {
          "target_id": entry_id,
          "phase": 26,
          "ops": ops
      }


  def verdict_to_patterns(verdict: str, include_unknown: bool) -> List[str]:
      verdict = verdict.lower().strip()
      if verdict in ("verified", "true", "min_true", "minimal_true"):
          return ["min_verified:true"]
      if verdict in ("refuted", "false", "min_false", "minimal_false"):
          return ["min_verified:false", "needs_review:true"]
      if verdict in ("unknown", "undecided") and include_unknown:
          return ["min_verified:unknown"]
      return []


  def main() -> None:
      ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
      ap.add_argument("--phase25", required=True, help="phase25_results.jsonl")
      ap.add_argument("--out", required=True, help="phase26_patches.jsonl")
      ap.add_argument("--include_unknown", action="store_true", help="also tag unknown with min_verified:unknown")
      ap.add_argument("--max_patches", type=int, default=0, help="0=all, else limit for testing")
      args = ap.parse_args()

      out_path = args.out
      if os.path.exists(out_path):
          # append mode; keep it explicit
          pass

      patches_written = 0

      for _, rec in iter_jsonl(args.phase25):
          # Expected Phase25 record schema (flexible):
          # { "id": "...", "status": "true/false/unknown" or "verified/refuted", "reason": "...", "witness": {...}
  }
          entry_id = rec.get("id") or rec.get("target_id")
          if not entry_id or not isinstance(entry_id, str):
              continue

          status = rec.get("status") or rec.get("verdict") or rec.get("minimality")
          if not isinstance(status, str):
              continue

          add_patterns = verdict_to_patterns(status, include_unknown=bool(args.include_unknown))
          if not add_patterns:
              continue

          reason = rec.get("reason")
          witness = rec.get("witness")
          checked = rec.get("checked_smaller")

          note_parts: List[str] = []
          if isinstance(reason, str) and reason.strip():
              note_parts.append(f"Phase26 minimality note: {reason.strip()}")
          if checked is not None:
              note_parts.append(f"checked_smaller={checked}")
          if witness is not None:
              # keep JSON compact but parseable
              try:
                  note_parts.append("witness=" + json.dumps(witness, ensure_ascii=False))
              except Exception:
                  note_parts.append("witness=<unserializable>")

          patch_note = "\n".join(note_parts) if note_parts else None

          patch = build_patch(entry_id=entry_id, add_patterns=add_patterns, patch_note=patch_note)
          write_jsonl(out_path, [patch])
          patches_written += 1

          if args.max_patches and patches_written >= args.max_patches:
              break

      print(f"[OK] wrote patches: {patches_written} -> {out_path}")


  if __name__ == "__main__":
      main()

╭────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────╮
│ x  Shell Command /usr/bin/env python3# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-"""Phase 26: Apply Phase25 minimality results as … │
│                                                                                                                │
│ Command exited with code 2.                                                                                    │
│ bash: -c: line 29: syntax error near unexpected token `('                                                      │

╰────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────╯

> 了解。**Phase 26
  =「Phase25の結果（phase25_results.jsonl）を読んで、KBへ“非破壊パッチ”として反映する」**フェーズです。
  あなたの要件どおり、
      •    verified → patterns に min_verified:true を付与（既存は保持、重複追加しない）
      •    refuted → patterns に needs_review:true を強化 + refutation の置換候補（witness）を 提案として
  patch_note に入れる（KB本文は壊さない）
      •    すべて 非破壊（ID変更なし・削除なし・追記のみ）

  を実装します。

  ⸻

  1) Phase26 実装コード

  tools/phase26_apply_minimality_patches.py

  """
  Phase 26: Apply Phase25 minimality results as non-destructive patches.

  Input:
    - phase25_results.jsonl : per-entry minimality verdicts
  Output:
    - phase26_patches.jsonl : patch operations (non-destructive)

  Patch policy:
    - verified => add patterns: "min_verified:true"
    - refuted  => add patterns: "needs_review:true", "min_verified:false"
                and store a patch_note with suggested replacement witness/structure
    - unknown  => add patterns: "min_verified:unknown" (optional; default off)

  We do NOT rewrite the KB directly here.
  Use your Phase19 patch applier to apply patches to foundation_kb.jsonl safely.
  """

  from __future__ import annotations

  import argparse
  import json
  import os
  from typing import Any, Dict, Iterable, List, Optional, Tuple


  def iter_jsonl(path: str) -> Iterable[Tuple[int, Dict[str, Any]]]:

              f.write(json.dumps(obj, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")

              "path": "/patch_note",

      ap.add_argument("--max_patches", type=int, default=0, help="0=all, else limit for testing")

          pass

          # { "id": "...", "status": "true/false/unknown" or "verified/refuted", "reason": "...", "witness": {...}
  }

          if checked is not None:
              note_parts.append(f"checked_smaller={checked}")
          if witness is not None:
              # keep JSON compact but parseable
              try:
                  note_parts.append("witness=" + json.dumps(witness, ensure_ascii=False))
              except Exception:
                  note_parts.append("witness=<unserializable>")

          patch_note = "\n".join(note_parts) if note_parts else None

          patch = build_patch(entry_id=entry_id, add_patterns=add_patterns, patch_note=patch_note)
          write_jsonl(out_path, [patch])
          patches_written += 1

          if args.max_patches and patches_written >= args.max_patches:
              break

      print(f"[OK] wrote patches: {patches_written} -> {out_path}")


  if __name__ == "__main__":
      main()


  ⸻

  2) 実行コマンド

  Phase25結果 → Phase26パッチ生成

  python3 tools/phase26_apply_minimality_patches.py \
    --phase25 avh_math/db/phase25_results.jsonl \
    --out    avh_math/db/phase26_patches.jsonl

  （unknown もタグ付けしたい場合）

  python3 tools/phase26_apply_minimality_patches.py \
    --phase25 avh_math/db/phase25_results.jsonl \
    --out    avh_math/db/phase26_patches.jsonl \
    --include_unknown


  ⸻

  3) KBへ反映（Phase19適用器がある前提）

  あなたがすでに持っている Phase19の安全なパッチ適用器で、
      •    対象KB: /Users/motonishikoudai/avh_math/avh_math/db/foundation_kb.jsonl
      •    パッチ: avh_math/db/phase26_patches.jsonl

  を適用します。

  例（あなたのPhase19のCLI名に合わせて読み替えてください）:

    --kb /Users/motonishikoudai/avh_math/avh_math/db/foundation_kb.jsonl \
    --patches avh_math/db/phase26_patches.jsonl \
    --backup


  ⸻

  4) これで何が実現するか（Phase26の成果）
      •    UI/検索側（Phase16/17）が min_verified:true をフィルタにできる
      •    needs_review:true が “最小性で落ちた”理由付きで可視化される
      •    refuted は patch_note に 置換候補witnessが入るので、次の Phase27（自動置換→再検証） に自然接続できる

  ⸻

  次の「次の実装」は、あなたの流れだとかなり自然に Phase27: 置換候補の自動適用→Phase22再検証（ループ）
  になります。
  そのまま Phase27 の設計＋コードも続けて出します。

✦ ファイルを保存して実行するためのスクリプトを作成します。Phase 25
  の出力形式に合わせて、データの読み込み部分を少し調整します。

✦ Phase 26 の処理が完了しました。

   1. Phase 25 結果の読み込み: phase25_runner.py の出力形式に対応した phase26_apply_patches.py を作成しました。
   2. パッチファイルの生成: phase25_results.jsonl から phase26_patches.jsonl を生成しました。スタブ検証の結果が
      unknown だったため、min_verified:unknown タグが付与されています。
   3. KBへの適用: 簡易的なパッチ適用スクリプトを用いて、foundation_kb.jsonl にパッチを適用（patterns
      へのタグ追加、patch_note の追記）しました。バックアップも作成済みです。

  これで Phase 26 が完了し、KB に検証結果（現状は unknown）が反映されました。
  次は Phase 27: 置換候補の自動適用と再検証ループ に進む準備が整いました。指示をお待ちします。
$/usr/bin/env python3
 # -*- coding: utf-8 -*-

 """
 Phase 27-A: Generate refutation replacement candidates for entries with
 min_verified:unknown or min_verified:false.

 Input:
   - KB JSONL (foundation_kb.jsonl)
 Output:
   - phase27_candidates.jsonl (one line per target with suggested candidate)

 Candidate is stored as a compact object:
   { target_id, domain, kind, candidate: { format, Domain, Structure, DroppedAssumption, FailurePoint,
 MinimalityHint, Witness } }

 This script does NOT modify KB.
 """

 from __future__ import annotations
 import argparse, json
 from typing import Any, Dict, Iterable, List, Tuple, Optional


 def iter_jsonl(path: str) -> Iterable[Tuple[int, Dict[str, Any]]]:
     with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
         for i, line in enumerate(f, start=1):
             line = line.strip()
             if not line:
                 continue
             yield i, json.loads(line)


 def has_pattern(entry: Dict[str, Any], p: str) -> bool:
     pats = entry.get("patterns")
     return isinstance(pats, list) and p in pats


 def get_domain(entry: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
     d = entry.get("domain")
     return d if isinstance(d, str) else ""


 # ---- Heuristic candidate generators (purely symbolic, GPU-free) ----

 def candidate_prop_logic(entry: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
     # Minimal truth-assignment style counterexample template
     return {
         "format": "BoundaryRefutationV1",
         "Domain": "propositional_logic",
         "Structure": "Valuation v over atoms; v: {p,q,r,...} -> {T,F}",
         "DroppedAssumption": "none (classical truth tables)",
         "FailurePoint": "Provide v such that premise(s)=T but conclusion=F",
         "MinimalityHint": "Use smallest atom set appearing in statement; 1-2 atoms if possible",
         "Witness": {
             "atoms": ["p", "q"],
             "valuation": {"p": "T", "q": "F"},
             "note": "Adjust atoms/values to falsify the target formula; keep atoms minimal."
         }
     }


 def candidate_fol(entry: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
     # Minimal finite structure template for quantifier-order failures etc.
     return {
         "format": "BoundaryRefutationV1",
         "Domain": "first_order_logic",
         "Structure": "Finite structure M with small domain (size 1..3), explicit interpretations",
         "DroppedAssumption": "none (standard FOL semantics)",
         "FailurePoint": "Quantifier order / Skolemization / satisfiable vs valid mismatch",
         "MinimalityHint": "Try |D|=1 then 2 then 3; prefer unary predicates / one binary relation",
         "Witness": {
             "domain": [0, 1],
             "predicates": {"P": [0], "Q": []},
             "relations": {"R": [[0, 1]]},
             "functions": {},
             "assignment": {"x": 0, "y": 1},
             "note": "Tune predicate/relations to make ∀∃ true but ∃∀ false, or vice versa."
         }
     }


 def candidate_model_theory(entry: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
     return {
         "format": "BoundaryRefutationV1",
         "Domain": "model_theory",
         "Structure": "Pair of elementarily equivalent but non-isomorphic structures, or finite-vs-infinite
 split",
         "DroppedAssumption": "categoricity / saturation / compactness misuse (identify which)",
         "FailurePoint": "Non-categoricity / non-standard model / LS theorem boundary",
         "MinimalityHint": "Use canonical examples: (Q,<) vs (R,<), non-standard PA, dense orders without
 endpoints",
         "Witness": {
             "example": "Dense linear orders: (Q,<) and (R,<) are elementarily equivalent but not isomorphic",
             "note": "Swap in standard counterexample family matching the statement tags/patterns."
         }
     }


 def candidate_modal(entry: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
     return {
         "format": "BoundaryRefutationV1",
         "Domain": "modal_logic",
         "Structure": "Small Kripke frame (W,R) with 1..3 worlds; valuation V",
         "DroppedAssumption": "toggle one frame property (reflexive/transitive/symmetric/euclidean/serial)",
         "FailurePoint": "Axiom schema fails on minimal frame when property is absent",
         "MinimalityHint": "Start with 2-world chain w0->w1; add edge for transitivity violations if needed",
         "Witness": {
             "worlds": ["w0", "w1"],
             "R": [["w0", "w1"]],
             "V": {"p": ["w1"]},
             "note": "Typical: falsify □p→p with non-reflexive frame, etc."
         }
     }


 def candidate_generic(entry: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
     return {
         "format": "BoundaryRefutationV1",
         "Domain": get_domain(entry) or "unknown_domain",
         "Structure": "Provide the smallest explicit structure that violates the claim",
         "DroppedAssumption": "identify the missing assumption that makes the claim fail",
         "FailurePoint": "state the exact sub-claim that fails under the structure",
         "MinimalityHint": "minimize domain/size/parameters; keep witness fully explicit",
         "Witness": {"note": "Fill explicit small structure; avoid placeholders."}
     }


 def build_candidate(entry: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
     d = get_domain(entry)
     if d == "propositional_logic":
         return candidate_prop_logic(entry)
     if d == "first_order_logic":
         return candidate_fol(entry)
     if d == "model_theory":
         return candidate_model_theory(entry)
     if d == "modal_logic":
         return candidate_modal(entry)
     return candidate_generic(entry)


 def main() -> None:
     ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
     ap.add_argument("--kb", required=True)
     ap.add_argument("--out", required=True)
     ap.add_argument("--limit", type=int, default=0, help="0=all, else limit for testing")
     args = ap.parse_args()

     written = 0
     with open(args.out, "a", encoding="utf-8") as fo:
         for _, e in iter_jsonl(args.kb):
             eid = e.get("id")
             if not isinstance(eid, str):
                 continue

             # target: unknown or false (from Phase26 tagging)
             if not (has_pattern(e, "min_verified:unknown") or has_pattern(e, "min_verified:false")):
                 continue

             # We focus on counterexample_schema first, but allow others if you want
             kind = e.get("kind")
             if kind not in ("counterexample_schema", "theorem", "axiom", "rule", "definition"):
                 continue

             cand = build_candidate(e)
             rec = {
                 "target_id": eid,
                 "domain": get_domain(e),
                 "kind": kind,
                 "candidate": cand
             }
             fo.write(json.dumps(rec, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
             written += 1
             if args.limit and written >= args.limit:
                 break

     print(f"[OK] phase27 candidates written: {written} -> {args.out}")


 if __name__ == "__main__":
     main()


 ⸻

 2) 候補をKBへ「非破壊パッチ」として適用（refutationは触らない）

 tools/phase27_candidates_to_patches.py

 #!/usr/bin/env python3
 # -*- coding: utf-8 -*-

 """
 Phase 27-B: Convert candidates to non-destructive patches.

 Input:
   - phase27_candidates.jsonl
 Output:
   - phase27_patches.jsonl

 Patch effect:
   - add patterns: "min_candidate:true"
   - set /refutation_candidate = <candidate object>
   - append patch_note about candidate generation
 """

 from __future__ import annotations
 import argparse, json, os
 from typing import Any, Dict, Iterable, Tuple


 def iter_jsonl(path: str) -> Iterable[Tuple[int, Dict[str, Any]]]:
     with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
         for i, line in enumerate(f, start=1):
             line = line.strip()
             if not line:
                 continue
             yield i, json.loads(line)


 def write_jsonl(path: str, rows: Iterable[Dict[str, Any]]) -> None:
     os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(path), exist_ok=True)
     with open(path, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
         for obj in rows:
             f.write(json.dumps(obj, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")


 def make_patch(target_id: str, candidate: Dict[str, Any], note: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
     return {
         "target_id": target_id,
         "phase": 27,
         "ops": [
             {"op": "add_unique", "path": "/patterns", "values": ["min_candidate:true"]},
             {"op": "set", "path": "/refutation_candidate", "value": candidate},
             {"op": "append_text", "path": "/patch_note", "value": note},
         ],
     }


 def main() -> None:
     ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
     ap.add_argument("--candidates", required=True)
     ap.add_argument("--out", required=True)
     ap.add_argument("--max", type=int, default=0)
     args = ap.parse_args()

     n = 0
     for _, rec in iter_jsonl(args.candidates):
         tid = rec.get("target_id")
         cand = rec.get("candidate")
         if not isinstance(tid, str) or not isinstance(cand, dict):
             continue
         note = "Phase27: generated refutation_candidate (non-destructive). Use Phase25 to re-verify
 minimality.\n"
         write_jsonl(args.out, [make_patch(tid, cand, note)])
         n += 1
         if args.max and n >= args.max:
             break

     print(f"[OK] phase27 patches written: {n} -> {args.out}")


 if __name__ == "__main__":
     main()

 ※ append_text は、あなたのPhase19適用器が未対応なら set に置き換えてOKです（patch_note を上書き）。
 その場合、append_text を set にして下さい。

 ⸻

 3) Phase27オーケストレータ（生成→パッチ→適用→再検証→再反映）

 tools/phase27_loop.sh

 #!/usr/bin/env bash
 set -euo pipefail

 KB="/Users/motonishikoudai/avh_math/avh_math/db/foundation_kb.jsonl"

 CAND="avh_math/db/phase27_candidates.jsonl"
 PATCH="avh_math/db/phase27_patches.jsonl"
 P25="avh_math/db/phase25_results.jsonl"
 P26="avh_math/db/phase26_patches.jsonl"

 echo "[Phase27] (1) generate candidates"
 python3 tools/phase27_generate_candidates.py --kb "$KB" --out "$CAND"

 echo "[Phase27] (2) candidates -> patches"
 python3 tools/phase27_candidates_to_patches.py --candidates "$CAND" --out "$PATCH"

 echo "[Phase27] (3) apply patches to KB (use your Phase19 applier)"
 # ↓ あなたの実際の適用器に合わせてコマンド名だけ調整して下さい
 python3 tools/phase19_apply_patches.py --kb "$KB" --patches "$PATCH" --backup

 echo "[Phase27] (4) re-run Phase25 minimality verification (your existing runner)"
 python3 tools/phase25_runner.py --kb "$KB" --out "$P25"

 echo "[Phase27] (5) Phase26 reflect results back to KB"
 python3 tools/phase26_apply_minimality_patches.py --phase25 "$P25" --out "$P26" --include_unknown
 python3 tools/phase19_apply_patches.py --kb "$KB" --patches "$P26" --backup

 echo "[OK] Phase27 loop complete"


 ⸻

 実行コマンド（Phase27）

 bash tools/phase27_loop.sh


 ⸻

 Phase27のポイント（設計意図）
 •KBの既存 refutation は壊さない（Phase27は“候補”を付けるだけ）
 •検証器（Phase25）が 候補を見て再判定できるようにするのが次の一手
 ※ もし Phase25 が refutation_candidate をまだ参照しない実装なら、次でそこを拡張します（Phase27.1）。

 ⸻

 次に出す「次の実装」

 あなたの現状（unknown多発）だと、次はほぼ確実にここが必要です：

 ✅ Phase25_runner.py を拡張して
 refutation_candidate がある場合は それを使って“より小さい構造”を優先検証する

 これを Phase27.1（Verifier wiring） として、すぐコードで出します。
 続けて出していいなら、このまま **phase25_runner.py の差分パッチ（追記だけで対応）**を出します。
╭────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────╮
│ x  Shell Command /usr/bin/env python3# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-"""Phase 27-A: Generate refutation replacement ca… │
│                                                                                                                │
│ Command exited with code 2.                                                                                    │
│ bash: -c: line 23: syntax error near unexpected token `('                                                      │
│ bash: -c: line 23: `def iter_jsonl(path: str) -> Iterable[Tuple[int, Dict[str, Any]]]:'                        │
╰────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────╯

> /usr/bin/env python3
  # -*- coding: utf-8 -*-

  """
  Phase 27-A: Generate refutation replacement candidates for entries with
  min_verified:unknown or min_verified:false.

  Input:
    - KB JSONL (foundation_kb.jsonl)
  Output:
    - phase27_candidates.jsonl (one line per target with suggested candidate)

  Candidate is stored as a compact object:
    { target_id, domain, kind, candidate: { format, Domain, Structure, DroppedAssumption, FailurePoint,
  MinimalityHint, Witness } }

  This script does NOT modify KB.
  """

  from __future__ import annotations
  import argparse, json
  from typing import Any, Dict, Iterable, List, Tuple, Optional


  def iter_jsonl(path: str) -> Iterable[Tuple[int, Dict[str, Any]]]:
      with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
          for i, line in enumerate(f, start=1):
              line = line.strip()
              if not line:
                  continue
              yield i, json.loads(line)


  def has_pattern(entry: Dict[str, Any], p: str) -> bool:
      pats = entry.get("patterns")
      return isinstance(pats, list) and p in pats


  def get_domain(entry: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
      d = entry.get("domain")
      return d if isinstance(d, str) else ""


  # ---- Heuristic candidate generators (purely symbolic, GPU-free) ----

  def candidate_prop_logic(entry: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
      # Minimal truth-assignment style counterexample template
      return {
          "format": "BoundaryRefutationV1",
          "Domain": "propositional_logic",
          "Structure": "Valuation v over atoms; v: {p,q,r,...} -> {T,F}",
          "DroppedAssumption": "none (classical truth tables)",
          "FailurePoint": "Provide v such that premise(s)=T but conclusion=F",
          "MinimalityHint": "Use smallest atom set appearing in statement; 1-2 atoms if possible",
          "Witness": {
              "atoms": ["p", "q"],
              "valuation": {"p": "T", "q": "F"},
              "note": "Adjust atoms/values to falsify the target formula; keep atoms minimal."
          }
      }


  def candidate_fol(entry: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
      # Minimal finite structure template for quantifier-order failures etc.
      return {
          "format": "BoundaryRefutationV1",
          "Domain": "first_order_logic",
          "Structure": "Finite structure M with small domain (size 1..3), explicit interpretations",
          "DroppedAssumption": "none (standard FOL semantics)",
          "FailurePoint": "Quantifier order / Skolemization / satisfiable vs valid mismatch",
          "MinimalityHint": "Try |D|=1 then 2 then 3; prefer unary predicates / one binary relation",
          "Witness": {
              "domain": [0, 1],
              "predicates": {"P": [0], "Q": []},
              "relations": {"R": [[0, 1]]},
              "functions": {},
              "assignment": {"x": 0, "y": 1},
              "note": "Tune predicate/relations to make ∀∃ true but ∃∀ false, or vice versa."
          }
      }


  def candidate_model_theory(entry: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
      return {
          "format": "BoundaryRefutationV1",
          "Domain": "model_theory",
          "Structure": "Pair of elementarily equivalent but non-isomorphic structures, or finite-vs-infinite
  split",
          "DroppedAssumption": "categoricity / saturation / compactness misuse (identify which)",
          "FailurePoint": "Non-categoricity / non-standard model / LS theorem boundary",
          "MinimalityHint": "Use canonical examples: (Q,<) vs (R,<), non-standard PA, dense orders without
  endpoints",
          "Witness": {
              "example": "Dense linear orders: (Q,<) and (R,<) are elementarily equivalent but not isomorphic",
              "note": "Swap in standard counterexample family matching the statement tags/patterns."
          }
      }


  def candidate_modal(entry: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
      return {
          "format": "BoundaryRefutationV1",
          "Domain": "modal_logic",
          "Structure": "Small Kripke frame (W,R) with 1..3 worlds; valuation V",
          "DroppedAssumption": "toggle one frame property (reflexive/transitive/symmetric/euclidean/serial)",
          "FailurePoint": "Axiom schema fails on minimal frame when property is absent",
          "MinimalityHint": "Start with 2-world chain w0->w1; add edge for transitivity violations if needed",
          "Witness": {
              "worlds": ["w0", "w1"],
              "R": [["w0", "w1"]],
              "V": {"p": ["w1"]},
              "note": "Typical: falsify □p→p with non-reflexive frame, etc."
          }
      }


  def candidate_generic(entry: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
      return {
          "format": "BoundaryRefutationV1",
          "Domain": get_domain(entry) or "unknown_domain",
          "Structure": "Provide the smallest explicit structure that violates the claim",
          "DroppedAssumption": "identify the missing assumption that makes the claim fail",
          "FailurePoint": "state the exact sub-claim that fails under the structure",
          "MinimalityHint": "minimize domain/size/parameters; keep witness fully explicit",
          "Witness": {"note": "Fill explicit small structure; avoid placeholders."}
      }


  def build_candidate(entry: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
      d = get_domain(entry)
      if d == "propositional_logic":
          return candidate_prop_logic(entry)
      if d == "first_order_logic":
          return candidate_fol(entry)
      if d == "model_theory":
          return candidate_model_theory(entry)
      if d == "modal_logic":
          return candidate_modal(entry)
      return candidate_generic(entry)


  def main() -> None:
      ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
      ap.add_argument("--kb", required=True)
      ap.add_argument("--out", required=True)
      ap.add_argument("--limit", type=int, default=0, help="0=all, else limit for testing")
      args = ap.parse_args()

      written = 0
      with open(args.out, "a", encoding="utf-8") as fo:
          for _, e in iter_jsonl(args.kb):
              eid = e.get("id")
              if not isinstance(eid, str):
                  continue

              # target: unknown or false (from Phase26 tagging)
              if not (has_pattern(e, "min_verified:unknown") or has_pattern(e, "min_verified:false")):
                  continue

              # We focus on counterexample_schema first, but allow others if you want
              kind = e.get("kind")
              if kind not in ("counterexample_schema", "theorem", "axiom", "rule", "definition"):
                  continue

              cand = build_candidate(e)
              rec = {
                  "target_id": eid,
                  "domain": get_domain(e),
                  "kind": kind,
                  "candidate": cand
              }
              fo.write(json.dumps(rec, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
              written += 1
              if args.limit and written >= args.limit:
                  break

      print(f"[OK] phase27 candidates written: {written} -> {args.out}")


  if __name__ == "__main__":
      main()


  ⸻

  2) 候補をKBへ「非破壊パッチ」として適用（refutationは触らない）

  tools/phase27_candidates_to_patches.py

  #!/usr/bin/env python3
  # -*- coding: utf-8 -*-

  """
  Phase 27-B: Convert candidates to non-destructive patches.

  Input:
    - phase27_candidates.jsonl
  Output:
    - phase27_patches.jsonl

  Patch effect:
    - add patterns: "min_candidate:true"
    - set /refutation_candidate = <candidate object>
    - append patch_note about candidate generation
  """

  from __future__ import annotations
  import argparse, json, os
  from typing import Any, Dict, Iterable, Tuple


  def iter_jsonl(path: str) -> Iterable[Tuple[int, Dict[str, Any]]]:
      with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
          for i, line in enumerate(f, start=1):
              line = line.strip()
              if not line:
                  continue
              yield i, json.loads(line)


  def write_jsonl(path: str, rows: Iterable[Dict[str, Any]]) -> None:
      os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(path), exist_ok=True)
      with open(path, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
          for obj in rows:
              f.write(json.dumps(obj, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")


  def make_patch(target_id: str, candidate: Dict[str, Any], note: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
      return {
          "target_id": target_id,
          "phase": 27,
          "ops": [
              {"op": "add_unique", "path": "/patterns", "values": ["min_candidate:true"]},
              {"op": "set", "path": "/refutation_candidate", "value": candidate},
              {"op": "append_text", "path": "/patch_note", "value": note},
          ],
      }


  def main() -> None:
      ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
      ap.add_argument("--candidates", required=True)
      ap.add_argument("--out", required=True)
      ap.add_argument("--max", type=int, default=0)
      args = ap.parse_args()

      n = 0
      for _, rec in iter_jsonl(args.candidates):
          tid = rec.get("target_id")
          cand = rec.get("candidate")
          if not isinstance(tid, str) or not isinstance(cand, dict):
              continue
          note = "Phase27: generated refutation_candidate (non-destructive). Use Phase25 to re-verify
  minimality.\n"
          write_jsonl(args.out, [make_patch(tid, cand, note)])
          n += 1
          if args.max and n >= args.max:
              break

      print(f"[OK] phase27 patches written: {n} -> {args.out}")


  if __name__ == "__main__":
      main()

  ※ append_text は、あなたのPhase19適用器が未対応なら set に置き換えてOKです（patch_note を上書き）。
  その場合、append_text を set にして下さい。

  ⸻

  2) 候補をKBへ「非破壊パッチ」として適用（refutationは触らない）

  #!/usr/bin/env python3

  """
  Phase 27-B: Convert candidates to non-destructive patches.

  Input:
    - phase27_candidates.jsonl
  Output:
    - phase27_patches.jsonl

  Patch effect:
    - add patterns: "min_candidate:true"
    - set /refutation_candidate = <candidate object>
    - append patch_note about candidate generation

                  continue
              yield i, json.loads(line)

      os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(path), exist_ok=True)

          for obj in rows:
              f.write(json.dumps(obj, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
